- 1 - 
個股報告

順德(2351) 
功率第一、均熱突圍，電源與散熱雙引擎同時引爆 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $410 
前次投資建議：2026.08.10 
「買進」 ，目標價: $252

報告日期：2026.09.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求回溫，均熱片新案發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，上修 EPS 至
15.2 元，考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調
升至 410 元(27X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 72.6%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/9 收盤價： 237.50 ➢ 展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自  AI 產品線，
目前逾 60 支新產品開案，在擴產效益帶動下，預期今
年 AI 相關營收倍數增長；均熱片近期已通過 4 間大
客戶認證，新增 4 台高噸位沖床與 15 台 CNC，並
年底達 100 萬片月產能、明年中達 2,000 萬片目標，
惟現階段量小、今年挹注不大。毛利率受惠  26H1 漲
價、稼動率高檔與產品組合轉佳，加上下半年客戶加價
搶產能，上修預估。綜上，兆豐投顧上修 2026年稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 
以上，產業加速朝 HVDC 架構演進，順德憑藉與歐美
大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度導線架成為
主要受惠者 ， AI 導線架營收可望隨  ± 400V/800V 
HVDC 滲透率提升而續強；均熱片進度優於研究部預
期，2,000 萬片有機會於 27 年中到位，隨 NPI 專案
逐步落地，27H2 動能將顯著增長，並隨 CPU/GPU 大
尺寸擴張成為新一輪成長主軸。在組合優化與稼動率維
持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 
年營收 206.4 億元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，
毛利率 24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增 2.8ppt，稅後淨利 
29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4 
元)，考量到今 明年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強
勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調升至410元(27x 
FY27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間72.6%。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967 年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，而在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始量產高功率導線架與穩壓器。
而順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散熱領域，並於 2026 年開始生產 CPO 
Switch產品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨，
預期均熱片月產能從 26年底 100萬片大幅拉升至 27年 2,000萬片(不含 NPI
產品需求規劃)，反映 AI導線架與均熱片需求大幅增長，順德於中國江蘇廠、
台灣彰化廠與南投廠同步擴產，並新購大噸位沖床，同時也在台灣與馬來西
亞找地新建廠房以因應未來幾年高壓電源與均熱片之高速成長期。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
AI 導線架供不應求，輔以銅價上漲與客戶加價搶購，上修 26Q3 EPS至 2.3 
元 
順德(2351)公告 8月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM -0.1%/YoY +61.2%，為單
月次高，Q3達成率 69%，遠優研究部預期，未能再創高係因消費性包括 VCM
與文具類產品較上月有所衰退，不過預期在車用需求全面復甦與 AI 導線架
供不應求，Q3營收可望再創高至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY +43.6%，車
用方面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將
延續，此外 AI導線架於 Q2有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將
逐月創高，而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，同時 AI 導線架供不應求使客
戶加價搶產能，故上修毛利率至 22%(原估 20%)，季增1.3ppt，年增9.6ppt，
同步上修稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +45.6%/YoY +405.2%，EPS 2.3 元 (原
估 1.8元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 60 支新產品開案，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年
AI 相關營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求
激增與新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩
年，近期已通過 4 間大客戶認證，除新增 4 台高噸位沖床外也同步增加 15
台 CNC 設備，同時也於近期租賃新廠房以滿足年底 100 萬片月產能需求，
並於明年中達成 2,000 萬片產能目標，公司放眼價值更高 NPI，研究部認為
產業最具指標之共平面度，順德已能做至 30μm 以下，名列產業前茅，預期
將受客戶大幅青睞，更大尺寸產品用於 ASIC/CPU/GPU 均熱片有望陸續驗
證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限，預期今年對營運挹注不大。毛利
率方面：受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，且
下半年客戶加價搶產能 ，上修毛利率預估。綜上所述，兆豐投顧上修順德  
2026 年營收 142 億元，YoY +3 8.3%，毛利率 21.3%，年增 8ppt，稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4元。

AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，
EPS 15.2 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，順德憑
藉與歐美大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為主要

- 4 - 
個股報告 
受惠者，預期在±400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動 AI導線架營收持續
高速增長；在均熱片方面，順德進度優於研究部預期，2,000 萬片有機會於
27年中到位，同時隨著 NPI專案逐步落地，27H2 營運動能將有顯著增長，
研究部認為均熱片可望隨著 CPU/GPU 更大尺寸擴張，成為新一輪成長主
軸；車用則在電動化與在地化趨勢下維持穩健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動
率維持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 年營收 206.4 億
元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，毛利率24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增2.8ppt，
稅後淨利 29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4 元)。

2382 廣達電腦(Quanta)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/08           研 究 員 ：曾 智 楷(Kevin  Tseng ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 、獲利創高， EPS 7.43 元 
廣達 2Q26 營收 1.04 兆， QoQ +28.1% ，YoY +105.6% ，受 惠於
伺 服 器、 筆電 第二季 均 呈 現雙 位 數 的成長 。 產 品 組 合 部 分 ， 伺 服
器 佔 整 體 營 收 8 成 ， 其 中 ，AI 伺 服 器 對 整 體 伺 服 器 營 收 貢 獻約
75%~80% ，筆電 則佔 整體營收的 低雙位數百分比。 筆電部分，
2Q26 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬 台 ，QoQ + 15% ，YoY  -5%。2Q26 筆
電 出 貨 優 於預 期 ， 主 要 由 於客 戶 新 機 種 的 拉 貨 動 能 延 續 至 第 二
季， 以及 各 品 牌 客 戶預期 記 憶 體 價 格 持 續 上 漲而 提 前 拉 貨。 而獲
利 方 面，2Q26 營 業 毛 利520 .8 億，QoQ +34.6 %。YoY + 46.6 %，
毛 利 率 5.0%，QoQ +0.2ppts，YoY -2.0ppts，營 業 利 益342 .4 億，
QoQ +48.7 %，YoY +67.8 %，營 利 率 3.3 %，QoQ +0.45 ppts，YoY 
-0.75 ppts 。公 司 表 示，高階 AI 機櫃對 公司 毛 利 的 稀 釋，隨著 產品
組合優化 影響 逐步 降 低 ， 以 及 2Q26 費 用 率 降 至 歷 史 新 低 的
1.72% ， 帶 動 營 利 率 獲 得 改 善 。2Q26 業 外 淨 利 23.2 億 ， 稅 後 淨
利 286.5 億， QoQ +35.2 %，YoY + 69.9 %， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.43
元。

二、 3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公司 公告 7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。展望 3Q26 ， 公司 預期 AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆 ，QoQ 
+1.5 %，YoY + 112.5 %， 毛 利 率 5.1%， 營 利 率 3.3%， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50 /31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但 由 於客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ，全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
投資評等：買進 
目標價格：440 元 
     14x 2027 EPS

2 
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率部分 ， 公司表示， 由於 下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調
今年 全 年 資 本 支 出 由 原 本 的300 億 元 至 400 億 元 ， 主 要 投 向 加 州
與 泰 國 AI 伺 服 器 產 能 。2026 年底 AI 產 能 相 較2025 年 底 達 成 翻
倍 成 長 的 目 標 維 持 不 變 。此 外 ， 先 前 公 告以 197 億 元 收 購 友 達 光
電 華 亞 廠，預 計 2027 年 第 四 季 完 成 交 割 點 交， 用 於2028 年台灣
AI 伺 服 器 產 能 倍 增 的 需 求 。預 估 公 司2026 全 年 營 收3.97 兆，YoY 
+87%，毛利率 5.0% ， 營 利 率 3.2%，稅後淨利 1062 億， YoY 
+41.7%，全 年 稅 後 EPS 2 7.50 元。預 估 公 司 2027 全 年 營 收5.04
兆，YoY + 27%，毛 利 率 4.8%，營 利 率 3.1%，稅 後 淨 利1221 億，
YoY + 15.0 %， 全 年 稅 後EPS 31.63 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 440 元 
受惠 於 AI 伺 服 器 的 強 勁 需 求 ， 公 司 維 持今 年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收三位
數成長 展 望，且隨 美 系 四 大CSP 資 本 支 出 展 望 維 持 正 向，GPU 、
ASIC 伺 服 器專案 持續 放量 ，將 有 望 挹 注 公 司 營 運 持 續 增 長，對 公
司 長 期 展 望 維 持 正 向 。以公司 202 7 年 EPS 來看 ， 目 前 股 價 交 易
於 10~11 倍 本 益 比 ， 評 價 仍 具 上 修 空 間 ， 給 予 買 進 投 資 建 議 ， 目
標 價 為 440 元(14x 202 7 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客
戶為 Tesla 、GM 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 鴻 海 、 緯 創 、 和 碩 、 仁 寶 電 腦
及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

二、預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 27.50/31.63 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公 司 表 示 ，AI 伺 服 器 訂 單 能 見 度 已到 2028 年，
維持 今年 全年 AI 伺 服 器 營 收 倍 增預期 不 變，而 通 用 伺 服 器亦 維持
全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可
維 持 在 與 上 半 年 相 當 的 水 準 。另 外 ， 在 產 能 佈 局 方 面 ， 公 司上調

### Page 1

1. Photonics IC scaling up: AI drives data transmission speed and bandwidth 
requirement, leading to strong demand on Photonics IC, requesting it to catch up 
Electrical IC automated production and testing ecosystem in 1 to 2 years, while EIC / 
semiconductors has been developed for decades. The strong demand brings 
opportunities to tool suppliers, across coupling and testing, which are necessary 
tools to accelerate PIC production. Robotechnik / FiconTEC describes the numbers of 
tools they target to ship in coming 1 year would be over 50% of what they’ve shipped 
in past 25 years, reflecting the strong demand and orders visibility.  
2. Testing - CPO earliest beneficiary: As a new technology, CPO naturally takes time 
and faces challenges in the path of commercialization; however, we identify testing 
equipment suppliers as the earliest beneficiaries, considering the expensive final 
module means multiple testing during the production process is necessary, given the 
earlier a failure is found, the lower cost a failure is. As a result, companies we visited 
continue to see Insertion 2 (double sided wafer-level testing) as necessary, and 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC launched the new generation in 3Q26, which fully complied 
with the global leading foundry’s communication infrastructure. 
3. Testing - shortening time with KGD (know good die) at early stage: To facilitate 
photonics IC scaling up, not only are more tools needed but better tools, requiring 
suppliers’ strong R&D and execution, delivering continuous specification upgrade. 
Robotechnik / FiconTEC, as global leaders in PIC testing, aim to shorten the 
wafer-level testing time by 50-60% next year, and target 4x faster die-level testing 
next year. Enlitech (7728.TWO, Not Covered) offers NightJar, a hyperspectral image 
system, revealing AWG (arrayed waveguide grating) optical loss pathways with 
absolute optical power mapping, allowing customers to KGD at Insertion 1 (PIC wafer

### Page 2

testing) or Insertion 0 (PIC wafer early defect screening), saving failure costs. 
4. Coupling - automated production becoming necessary in high-speed connection: 
AI drives optical module towards high speed and high density / bandwidth, driving 
demand on automated production. GMT (4573.TWO, Not Covered) showcased dual FA 
active alignment equipment for 1.6T optical module, which could do the coupling for 
both Tx and Rx simultaneously, saving 50% of coupling time compared to traditional 
equipment. FitTech (6706.TW, Not Covered) showcased optical engine assembly system, 
featuring with passive / active alignment and customized development of coupling 
algorithms, which could be used in multiple products: high-speed optical modules, ELS, 
OCS, NPO, CPO, SiPh and optical chiplets. ASMPT showcased MEGA multi-chip bonding 
solution for complex multi-chip and photonics packaging applications, combining 
high-precision placement with adhesive dispensing, UV fixation and 3D inspection in one 
platform. 
5. FAU & Laser specification upgrade: With optical networking expanding from scale 
out to scale up, FAU and laser specification is also migrating. We estimate FOCI‘s FAU in 
2026E to be mainly 40 channels (starting in 3Q26) for 3.2T, followed by 80 channels 
(within 2026E) for 6.4T, and 100 channels under R&D. The company’s FAU enjoys several 
customers across fabless, foundry, laser suppliers, etc. FOCI Aug revenues accelerated to 
+20% MoM (vs. +9% MoM in Jul), reflecting the ramp up of high-end FAU. VPEC‘s 
optoelectronics revenues enjoy strong growth on the back of strong CW lasers demand 
and InP substrate supply getting better. Its InP epiwafer for CW lasers is also migrating 
from 70mW / 100mW (scale out) to above 100mW (scale up, NPO) and 300mW / 
400mW (scale up, CPO), along with expansion from CW lasers to 100G / 200G EML, 
supporting its growth ahead. 
6. AI drives CMP slurry: Anji Micro owns a comprehensive customer base, allowing it to 
ride on growing AI spending across China and overseas. The global-tier AI infrastructure 
continues to migrate, driving the CMP slurry consumption per system, along with AI 
server racks ramp up, enlarging the total addressable market. As the CMP slurry local 
leader, we are positive on Anji’s capability to offer qualified and reliable semi material 
products to penetrate global-tier clients.   
Company highlights

Robotechnik (300757.SZ, Buy, 12m TP at Rmb796) Chairman & Head of FiconTest 
visit: We hosted Robotechnik Chairman and Head of FiconTest and visited the booth in 
Semicon Taiwan on Sep 3. Management of Robotechnik highlights the strong demand on 
Photonics IC automated production and testing, driving their tools demand. The 
company’s strong beat in 2Q26 results (report link) reaffirms our positive view on 
Robotechnik, riding on the growing trend across CPO, NPO, OCS, and optical modules. 
We identify Robotechnik to be the major beneficiary in the photonics IC scaling trend, 
considering its comprehensive tools coverage and strong automation capability across 
software and hardware. Read more: Robotechnik / FiconTEC Semicon Taiwan 
takeaways.

Price target methodology and risks - ASMPT
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral-rated on ASMPT with a 12-month target price
of HK$177, which is based on 24.9x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E).
Our target P/E is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and
OPM.
Key upside/downside risks: 1) customers’ faster-/slower-than-expected adoption of
Advanced Packaging tools; 2) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand from

automotive customers; and 3) stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for traditional
IC packaging and SMT equipment.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Anji Micro
Valuation: We are Buy-rated on Anji Micro with a 12-month target price of Rmb509,
which is based on 46x 2030E discounted P/E (discounted back to 2027E). Our target P/E
is derived from correlation between peers P/E to the sum of NI YoY and OPM.
Key downside risks: 1) supply chain risks; 2) weaker semi client demand; and 3)

weaker-than-expected product expansion.
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of NT$833 is based on a 15.4x 2030E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 12.6%. Our target multiple is derived from peers’
correlation between forward-year trading P/E and earnings growth.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition, technology migration
trends in optical networking, and slower-than-expected capacity ramp-up.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC

Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is
based on a 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics
supply chain.
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - RoboTechnik
Valuation: Our 12-month target price of Rmb796 is based on a 20.7x 2031E P/E,
discounted back to 2027E at a COE of 11.3%. Our target multiple is derived from the
correlation between consumer electronic peers’ PEG&M (2026E P/E to 2027E NI YoY and
OPM), with RoboTechnik’s 2032E net income at 2% YoY and 2032E OPM at 32%.
Key downside risks: Fiercer-than-expected market competition; lower-than-expected

optical connection end demand; worse-than-expected photovoltaic market downcycle.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

n Slower-than-expected AI demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: Aug sales +49% MoM, aided 
by solid AI momentum; lift PO to NT$265

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 265.00 TWD | Price: 189.00 TWD

Aug’26 sales +49% MoM, mainly driven by AI 
Wistron’s Aug’26 sales of NT$460bn grew 49% MoM (+166% YoY), with subsidiary 
Wiwynn’s Aug sales of NT$144bn grew 23% MoM (+50% YoY). Wistron’s strong Aug 
sales were mainly driven by enterprise AI server projects, with overall AI server rack 
shipments roughly doubling MoM. Given the strong Aug sales, we lift our 3Q26 sales 
forecast by 11%. We expect Sep sales to decline 20% MoM to NT$370bn from the high 
Aug base. Yet, Sep sales should remain above July’s NT$308bn as we stay positive on AI 
order momentum into following quarters, driven by Nvidia’s new Vera Rubin generation.  
PC remains soft; Aug shipments down 13% MoM 
Wistron’s Aug NB shipments were 1.4mn (-13% MoM, -36% YoY). We view it as in line 
with supply chain’s commentary on weaker demand in 2H26 amid continued component 
cost inflation issue. This also echoes Wistron management’s cautious tone that PC 
shipment should decline 10-20% HoH in 2H26 following earlier order pull-in 1H26. We 
now expect 3Q26 NB shipment to fall 18% QoQ, implying 1.5mn units of NB shipments 
in Sep (+8% MoM, -31% YoY) with potential quarter-end pull-in demand. 
Lift PO to NT$265; reiterate Buy on solid AI momentum 
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 4-6% to reflect higher sales, mainly driven by AI (with 
diversifying clients/projects), better operating leverage, and actual 2Q26 data. We raise 
our PO to NT$265 from NT$238, with our valuation method unchanged at 15x 2H26-
1H27E EPS. We reiterate Buy on Wistron’s solid multi-year AI server outlook. We expect 
a positive near-term market reaction to the strong Aug sales.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU server rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$265 is based on 15x 2H26-1H27E EPS. We use 15x valuation multiple 
(around 1.5SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating) 
Ratings dispersion guidelines for coverage clusterR2 
Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70% 
Neutral ≥ 0% ≤ 30% 
Underperform N/A ≥ 20% 
R2Ratings dispersions may vary from time to time where BofA Global Research believes it better reflects the investment prospects of stocks in a Coverage Cluster. 
INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a c ommon industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

### Page 2

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•2027 Pricing Looks More Favorable.  Management hopes to implement broader-based price increases across AI and
non-AI products to offset higher wafer, energy and other input costs and support new capacity investments. Pricing
should therefore turn more positive in 2027 if utilization remains high. Management noted that every 1% ASP
increase could improve gross margin by roughly 0.5–0.6ppt. However, margins are unlikely to revisit the >45%

COVID-era peak because depreciation is structurally higher following the current investment cycle.
•Intel 12nm cooperation: Capital-Light Path, contribution starting in late 2027 . The Intel collaboration is
strategically important because it gives UMC a capital-light path from its strong 22/28nm franchise into 12nm. Intel
provides existing Arizona manufacturing equipment and capacity, while UMC contributes technology conversion, RD
resources and its existing customer relationships. Initial customers are primarily Wi-Fi and communications-related,

with the first meaningful revenue expected around late 2027; broader contribution should build gradually thereafter
as customers validate the first wave. UMC expects to book the wafer revenue, with Intel manufacturing costs flowing
through UMC’s cost structure and economics broadly shared between the two parties. Expansion into smaller nodes
or advanced packaging with Intel remains possible, but management stressed that 12nm must first demonstrate

competitive yield, manufacturing performance and profitability.
•Silicon Photonics: Singapore Fab Becomes a Major AI Growth Platform.  The company is building dedicated 12-inch
capacity for silicon photonics in Singapore, with the new facility expected to be ready in roughly 14 months.  The
platform can encompass PICs and related components such as TIA, with management describing demand as
stronger than its available engineering resources.  UMC also has exposure to TFLN through an affiliate focused

initially on 6-inch technology, with potential migration to 8-inch manufacturing supported by UMC. Current
economics are attractive because photonics wafers can command multiples of conventional CMOS wafer pricing.
Longer term, UMC wants to combine silicon photonics, specialty materials and packaging capabilities into an
integrated platform.
•Advanced Packaging: Building an Open-Ecosystem Second Source. Advanced packaging represents UMC’s third
major structural growth driver. Rather than competing directly with TSMC’s fully integrated leading-edge ecosystem,

UMC aims to become a credible second-source, open-ecosystem provider. Its developing capabilities include
interposers, wafer-to-wafer and hybrid bonding, DTC, and related packaging processes, with silicon photonics
potentially integrated into the same broader solution. UMC is constructing two new advanced-packaging facilities in
Taiwan. Construction is expected to take more than two years, while existing facilities can provide limited capacity
during the interim.

•Specialty Strategy Provides Defense Against China Competition. UMC continues to differentiate itself through
specialty technologies rather than competing primarily on commodity mature-node capacity. Management
estimates 50–60%+ of revenue now comes from specialty processes, which provides meaningful insulation from
Chinese foundry pricing pressure. China’s semiconductor market is also becoming increasingly segmented into
“China-for-China” and “non-China-for-non-China” supply chains, limiting direct cross-border price competition.

UMC’s two China fabs remain highly utilized and represent roughly 10% of group capacity. Overall, UMC’s strategy is
shifting from being primarily a mature-node foundry toward a specialty and heterogeneous-integration platform
while improving utilization and pricing provide a stronger cyclical backdrop for 2027.

### Page 4

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
4
UMC
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 15x 2027E EPS, which is higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We
believe our target multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's
increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche
technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry peers. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E PB of

3.5x/3.2x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in
28nm; 3) worse-than-expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a significant decline in smartphone
end-demand; 5) poor contribution from Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from Chinese peers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

Presenters: Wendell Huang, SVP & CFO  
Bottom Line: We have three key takeaways: (1) TSMC continues to view AI as a 
multi-year megatrend that remains at a very early stage, with lower compute costs 
expected to accelerate AI adoption and ultimately drive greater demand for 
advanced silicon; (2) execution in Arizona continues to progress well, with Phase 1 
achieving yields comparable to Taiwan and already generating profits, while TSMC 
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside 
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona; and (3) despite dilution from the N2 ramp 
and overseas expansion, management remains comfortable achieving its long-term 
gross margin target of 56% and above, supported by disciplined capacity planning, 
high utilization, productivity improvements, cost reductions, and pricing that reflects 
TSMC’s value. 
Key Takeaways

AI remains in the early stages of a multi-year megatrend: TSMC continues to n
see increasing adoption of AI. Management also highlighted the shift from 
generative AI toward agentic AI, which should broaden compute demand 
beyond accelerators to CPUs. TSMC remains confident in the durability of the AI 
cycle based on discussions not only with its direct customers but also with its 
customers’ customers. Recent visits of US CSPs further reinforced management’s 
confidence, as cloud service providers appeared well prepared in areas including 
power and broader infrastructure deployment. 
Lower compute costs should drive greater consumption and silicon demand: n
Management believes open-source models and continued improvements in 
computing efficiency will reduce token costs, leading to greater AI 
consumption and broader adoption, supporting incremental demand for 
advanced silicon. TSMC has already incorporated this dynamic into its capacity 
planning and continues to expand aggressively to support what it views as

### Page 2

structural, multi-year demand growth.
Disciplined demand validation remains critical amid aggressive expansion: n
TSMC uses a combination of top-down and bottom-up analysis to validate demand.
Its top-down assessment considers macroeconomic conditions, semiconductor
demand, fabless/system companies, the foundry industry, etc, while its bottom-up
approach evaluates demand across different segments and incorporates feedback
from customers and customers’ customers. Management acknowledged that

semiconductors will remain cyclical, but believes correctly identifying the underlying
megatrend is more important than attempting to forecast every short-term
fluctuation.
Arizona execution is demonstrating that TSMC’s manufacturing model can n
travel: TSMC reiterated that overseas expansion is based on customer demand for
geographic flexibility and an appropriate level of government support. Arizona
Phase 1, using N4 technology, is in operation, with yields comparable to Taiwan,

satisfactory quality and reliability, and positive profitability. Management views
this as an important milestone because it demonstrates internally and to customers
that TSMC can produce wafers in the US with the same quality as in Taiwan.
Equipment move-in for Phase 2, which will use N3 technology, is expected to begin
soon; construction of Phase 3 has started; and preparation work for Phase 4 and the
first US advanced-packaging facility is underway.

Arizona has substantial room for further expansion: In addition to its original n
Arizona development, TSMC has acquired another parcel of land capable of
accommodating an additional five to six fabs, supporting its recently
announced incremental US$100bn investment. Based on its current plan, TSMC
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona. However, the most advanced technologies

will continue to ramp first in Taiwan because the early-stage technology transfer
requires close collaboration and frequent movement of engineers among R&D and
manufacturing sites. Overseas deployment will generally follow after the technology
has stabilized in Taiwan.
Customers increasingly value and are willing to pay for geographic flexibility: n
Management noted that customers initially requested only a modest amount of
geographically diversified supply, but their requirements have increased as TSMC has

demonstrated successful execution in Arizona. TSMC believes this geographic
flexibility represents incremental value and intends to reflect that value in its pricing,
with customers increasingly willing to recognize the associated premium.
Technology, manufacturing excellence, capacity, and trust underpin TSMC’s n
competitive moat: Management believes customers select a foundry partner based
on access to the best technology, the ability to ramp rapidly into high-volume

manufacturing with attractive economics, sufficient capacity, and confidence that
the foundry will not compete with its customers. TSMC is confident in maintaining
this lead by continuing to advance its roadmap. Management also highlighted its
record of rapidly ramping new technologies, the scale of its wafer and
advanced-packaging investments, and its consistent delivery against customer
10 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

commitments as key elements of customer trust that cannot be replicated through
CapEx or engineering recruitment alone.
Pricing remains strategic rather than opportunistic: TSMC does not intend to raise n
prices simply because near-term utilization is high, nor reduce prices simply because
utilization temporarily weakens. Instead, the company’s approach is to price
sustainably and consistently based on the value it provides, while ensuring that
customers can remain successful over the long term. Management noted that

TSMC’s structural profitability has continued to improve, with its long-term gross
margin target increasing from 50% five years ago to 53% and now 56% and above.
Leading-edge wafer and advanced-packaging capacity remain tight: n
Management continues to build capacity wherever possible to meet customer
demand. N3 demand remains particularly strong: unlike prior technology cycles,
when demand for a node typically began declining two to three years after its

introduction, TSMC is adding further N3 capacity and still does not expect supply
to be sufficient. The company is also expanding advanced-packaging capacity to
address strong accelerator demand. Outside TSMC, management identified power
and memory as potential bottlenecks cited by other parts of the AI supply chain,
while emphasizing that foundry capacity itself remains difficult to expand because
fabs require two to three years to build and another one to two years to ramp.

TSMC’s disclosed AI revenue understates its broader exposure: Management n
reiterated that its disclosed AI revenue definition is relatively narrow and primarily
captures GPUs, custom AI accelerators and memory base die. It excludes CPUs and
networking products because TSMC cannot reliably determine whether each chip is
ultimately deployed in an AI system. As agentic AI drives incremental demand across
x86, Arm, and RISC-V CPUs, as well as networking and other supporting silicon,

TSMC’s total AI-related exposure is therefore broader than its reported
AI-accelerator revenue. The company intends to retain its existing definition until it
can more accurately distinguish the end use of these products.
No visible barrier to continued transistor scaling: TSMC is currently ramping N2, n
with A14 expected approximately two years later, while development of the
subsequent node is already underway. Management does not currently see a

technical issue preventing delivery of the next node or continued advancement
beyond the existing roadmap. TSMC views close collaboration with customers as a
key foundry advantage, allowing the company to develop each technology against
specific customer performance, power, density, and economic requirements.
Cross-node capacity optimization is an underappreciated advantage: TSMC n
highlighted its ability to generate additional output through productivity

improvements and to convert tools and capacity across technology nodes. For
example, the company can use portions of its N5 capacity to support N3 demand
and can also optimize capacity across less-adjacent nodes where technically
feasible. Management views this cross-node flexibility as a distinctive element of
TSMC’s manufacturing excellence, allowing the company to respond more
quickly to demand changes while reducing the risk of capacity obsolescence.

Advanced packaging is strategic primarily because it enables wafer growth: n
10 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

CoWoS capacity remains tight, and TSMC already started outsourcing selected 
packaging processes to OSAT partners to support additional growth. Management 
emphasized that advanced packaging is intended primarily to ensure and enable 
wafer demand rather than serve as a standalone profit pool. TSMC is therefore 
developing multiple packaging architectures, including panel-level packaging, etc, 
while remaining open to customers using alternative packaging providers, as either 
approach ultimately supports demand for TSMC wafers. 
Advanced-packaging execution remains a key differentiator: TSMC noted that its n
current advanced-packaging capability supports approximately 5.5x reticle size, 
with a roadmap to reach approximately 14x reticle sizes within the next several 
years. While competing roadmaps may also appear attractive, management believes 
execution and the ability to deliver in high-volume manufacturing will ultimately 
determine competitive outcomes. TSMC does not believe competitors can use 
advanced-packaging wins to meaningfully displace its leading-edge wafer 
business, as foundry and packaging remain fundamentally different businesses. 
A unified TSMC culture supports overseas manufacturing execution: TSMC’s n
objective is not to create separate Taiwan and US cultures, but to establish a 
consistent TSMC culture centered on integrity, commitment, innovation, and 
customer trust (ICIC). Locally hired Arizona employees spend six to eighteen months 
training and working in Taiwan, while experienced Taiwan-based employees are 
assigned to Arizona to work alongside local teams. Over time, the number of Taiwan 
assignees should decline as locally trained employees assume greater responsibility. 
TSMC is applying this approach across its overseas manufacturing sites. 
Management remains comfortable with its 56%-and-above long-term gross n
margin target: TSMC reiterated its 6 major drivers of profitability: new-technology 
ramp, pricing, cost, product mix, utilization, and FX. The N2 ramp is expected to 
dilute 2026 GM by approximately 2-3ppts, but its profitability should improve over 
time toward the corporate average; management also noted that N2’s structural 
profitability is better than N3 at a comparable stage. Overseas expansion is 
expected to dilute GM by 2-3ppts during the early portion of the current five-year 
period, then widening to 3-ppts later as more overseas fabs begin production. Each 
new fab will initially create dilution before improving along its learning curve. Taking 
these puts and takes together, management remains very comfortable that 
disciplined capacity planning, healthy utilization, cost reduction, productivity 
gains, and pricing that reflects TSMC’s value will enable the company to deliver 
for shareholders and achieve its long-term gross margin target ‘of 56% and 
higher’ with focus on the ‘higher’ side. 
Valuation/Risks

TSMC (Buy): Our 12-month price target is NT$3,100, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12-month price 
target of US$620, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. Key

downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

AI semi capex, in particular for compute chips, should outgrow
overall AI capex in 2028. We add cross-checks on ASICs after
AVGO’s guidance and the Qualcomm/AWS AI ASIC
announcement.
Will 2028 be another growth year for AI semis? Our US internet analyst Brian
Nowak published the Morgan Stanley AI Guidebook: Navigating the Age of
Inference, and suggests 12% CSP capex growth into 2028, raising investor questions
about AI semi growth momentum. Based on our latest supply chain checks, we

introduce preliminary 2028 CoWoS/CoPoS/EMIB-T capacity assumptions. In
aggregate, we still see 50% capacity growth in 2028 (see Exhibit 1), implying
flattish infrastructure (e.g., the shell and AI DC) spending and moderate memory
pricing in 2028.
Larger chip size and more applications (CPU, networking) to drive further
growth in 2028: All these packaging technologies are 2.5D (compute and memory
chips on side to side) for AI/HPC chips, including AI GPU, XPU, CPU, and networking.

•CoWoS (wafer based): We still see some CoW expansion at TSMC. For oS, TSMC
may need more partners than ASE. Our checks suggest both Amkor and Xintec
(non-covered) will diversify oS production in the US and Taiwan, respectively.
• CoPoS (panel-based): This is a panel-level process. After converting to 12-inch
CoWoS wafer equivalents, we assume 5–10kwpm in 2028. The initial target project
should be Nvidia’s Feynman Ultra GPU.
• EMIB-T (substrate-based): Although a substrate-based technology, we convert it

to 12-inch CoWoS wafer-equivalent capacity as well. One EMIB-T substrate can
generate 16 pcs of a 9x reticle-size chip, around 4x the chips a CoWoS wafer can
generate. Our checks indicate ~40k–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028,
producing at least 2mn of Humufish (9x reticle) substrates. 40k–45k wafer
equivalents also imply 10-15% share in 2.5D packaging.
Read-across from recent ASIC guidance at Broadcom and the Qualcomm/AWS
partnership

•According to Joe Moore’s report: Broadcom Inc.: Earnings solid; raising FY 28 EPS,
more specific FY27 revenue guidance is now US$115 bn, and that could double again
in FY28. Our CoWoS supply-chain checks already see an AVGO AI lab customer
booking ~15k–30k CoWoS wafers for 2027.
•Qualcomm announced its ASIC partnership with AWS, a key Alchip ASIC customer.
However, the press release points to inference, different from Alchip’s Trainium4,
which covers both training and inference. We therefore see limited impact to Alchip.

and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

We introduce preliminary 2028 CoWoS/CoPoS/EMIB-T capacity assumptions. In aggregate,
we still see 50% capacity growth in 2028 (see Exhibit 1 ). All these packaging technologies
are 2.5D for AI/HPC chips, including AI GPU, XPU, CPU, and networking.
Our US internet analyst Brian Nowak published Morgan Stanley AI Guidebook: Navigating

the Age of Inference, and suggests 12% CSP capex growth into 2028, raising investor
questions about AI semi growth momentum.
On ASIC industry, we continue to see a growing chance for MediaTek to win a second AI
CSP or AI lab customer via its Nvidia NVLink Fusion partnership. The decision could be
made in 4Q26, which could serve as a potential significant  catalyst for MediaTek.

TSMC CoWoS and CoPoS
With continual booming demand in AI, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to expedite
capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We expect TSMC's CoWoS capacity to further build
to 260kwpm in 2028 from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion mainly

focusing in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial
production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. As long as the progress goes well, we expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with the location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.
We expect the non-TSMC camp to expand CoWoS capacity to 110kwpm by 2028 year-end
from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from
30kwpm at 2027-end to 50-60kwpm at 2028-end, with expansion focused on CoWoS-L

and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-
end to 55-60kwpm at 2028-end, also focused on CoWoS-L and CoWoS-R.

to 260kwpm in 2028, with OSAT (ASE/SPIL/Amkor) together
building to 110kwpm in the same year

For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly
decrease to 95kwpm in 2027 as it's converting some capacities toward EMIB-T. We see the
major customers of EMIB-M being Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server
CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale
to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is
converted for  12-inch wafer equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to

communicate and compared with TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T
could still  produce close to 3mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5
chips per wafer of EMIB-T.

see a slower ramp of AMD Venice CPU which should be the major customer of ASE and

CoWoS update (Exhibit 10):
• AMD's total 2027 CoWoS consumption increases 165% Y/Y to 345k:
° We expect AI GPU-related production to be all at TSMC CoWoS, while
MI455/450 would be the key focus for 2027, with minor production for MI500
series (chip code name Arcadia) in late 2027. Both MI455 and MI450 could
reach around 500-650k units respectively. We estimate total TSMC CoWoS-L
booking increases 200% Y/Y in 2027, to 210k.
° We see the non-TSMC camp, including ASE/SPIL and  Amkor, being mainly in

charge of Venice CPU, high-end PC CPU, and gaming GPU production.
Powertech was also planning for the production of Venice, but we now expect
it to focus on gaming GPU only, given the lower yield rate of panel level
packaging.
° However, given the slower production  ramp at OSAT,   we believe TSMC would

also need to support around 80k CoWoS-L for Venice production, partially
squeezing out the capacity of AI GPU build.

210k in 2027, implying 4.4mn units of CPU production, lower than our initial
forecast of 5.6mn.
• Microsoft's Maia200 CoWoS booking increases from 1k in 2026e to 5k in 2027, we
estimate:
° We have been seeing upward revision demand of Microsoft Maia 200 into

2027; we believe the demand could reach around 5k of CoWoS in 2027,
suggesting around 100-150k chips.
• US AI Lab CoWoS booking could reach 15-30k in 2027:
° With the recent announcement of AI ASIC from one US AI lab, we expect its
CoWoS booking at TSMC to reach 15-30k in 2027, with support from
Broadcom.

Connecting Nvidia GPU shipments and rack shipments:
In Howard Kao’s rack shipment update, Greater China Technology Hardware: GB200 and
GB300 NVL72 Racks in August 2026 (9 Sep 2026), August racks recovered, implying
more Blackwell GPU consumption. His CY26 full-year rack forecast is now at 80-85K
racks. This is similar to our observation that KYEC continues final testing for meaningful
volumes of B-series GPUs in 3Q26.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

3-month preview: SZS Aug revenues were down 10% YoY, or up 1% MoM to NT$820m, 
while 38% below our estimates, and we attribute it to the muted growth of wearable 
hinges and Notebook hinges. However, we remain positive on company’s new projects, 
and expects to see MoM/ QoQ growth recovery ahead on clients’ new products launch 
and better seasonality to reflect on company’s growth.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in SZS Aug revenues, revise down 2026E earnings by 11%, 
revise up 2027E earnings by 1%, and keep 2028E earnings largely unchanged. We revise 
down 2026E revenues mainly on lower-than-expected Aug revenues, considering longer 
time for new projects to reflect on company’s revenues/ earnings. We revise up 2027 
earnings mainly on higher revenues, reflecting our positive view on SZS as the key 
beneficiary of rising foldable phones.

Valuation: We are Buy rated on SZS with a 12-month target price of NT$300 (vs. 
previously at NT$286) on lower earnings. Our TP is based on a 21.9x 2027E P/E (vs. 
previously at 21.0x), which is derived from the updated sector correlation of 2027E P/E 
vs. 2027E-28E EPS YoY (Exhibit 8), with re-rating supported by positive view on rising 
foldable phone penetration. The 21.9x 2027E P/E is also within SZS’s trading range since 
Aug 2019.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - SZS 
Valuation: We use a near-term P/E vs. EPS growth correlation to derive our target price 
for SZS, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our target 2027E P/E multiple 
of 21.9x for SZS is based on the sector correlation of 2027E P/E vs. 2027-28E earnings 
growth. We thus derive our 12-month target price at NT$300. We are Buy rated. Key 
risks: Weaker-than-expected headset hinges demand; slower-than-expected foldable 
phone penetration; potential competition from more suppliers; soft market demand.

between P/E and NI YoY

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this

Shipments -19% vs. MSe;
Lowering 3Qe by 7%,
Introducing 4Qe Builds
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen

Andy Meng, CFA

Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall August ODM notebook shipments came in 19% below our estimate: Pull-
forward demand appears to have come to an end, channel partners and distributors
have stocked up on inventory, and OEMs are slowing their sales into the channel.
We believe this is the main reason why August NB builds came in slower than our

expectation. However, we continue to expect product mix to continue to shift
toward higher-end, more-profitable models. Several ODMs such as Quanta and
Compal have even lowered 3Q NB build forecasts compared to their earlier guidance
on 2Q earnings calls three weeks ago. Thus we lower our 3Q NB build estimate by
7% to 25.4M units (-16% q/q, -25% y/y). This sequential decline is sub-seasonal vs.
the 15-year average seasonality of +5% q/q in 3Q, and implies September NB builds

at 9.9M units (+35% m/m, -18% y/y).
We introduce our 4Q notebook build estimate at 23.9M units (-6% q/q, -26% y/
y): This sequential decline is sub-seasonal (despite an already lowered base in 3Q)
vs. the 15-year average seasonality of flat q/q in 4Q. None of the ODMs have
provided official guidance for 4Q notebook builds, but our preliminary checks
suggests that they will continue to fall q/q. On our estimate, this implies that the
top 5 Taiwan NB ODMs' builds for CY26 will come in at ~108.8M units (-16% y/

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' August 2026 NB shipments were 7.3M units (-11% m/m, -35% y/y),
19% below our estimate.
We see September 2026 NB builds at 9.9M units (+35% m/m, -18% y/y).
We lower our 3Q26 NB build estimate by 7% to 25.4M units (-16% q/q, -25% y/y).
We introduce our 4Q26 NB build estimate at 23.9M units (-6% q/q, -26% y/y).

Our top five ODMs' CY26 NB build estimate is ~108.8M units (-16% y/y).
September 14, 2026 12:00 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
APAC Tech
TW Summit Day 1 on Hardware/Components
Chassis/rail kit suppliers have started to ramp for new server platform
One chassis supplier has noted that, post solid inventory built in Q2, ODMs have slowed
down pull-in momentum for one of the major US CSP ASIC suppliers (likely AWS, in our
view) in Q3 due to supply bottleneck for memory and cooling components. Inventories
are anticipated to be digested by September and anticipate ODM pull-in momentum to

resume in September/October. Another supplier noted AWS general server volumes
were also down in Q2 on component mismatch (citing memory, CPU, and MLCC
shortages), but seeing shipment coming back from Q3. Meanwhile, one supplier noted
it has begun to ramp chassis shipments for NVDA Vera Rubin platform with enterprise
started pull-in earlier in Q226, followed by major CSPs' orders in Q326; and another
supplier noted it anticipates to start shipping VR rail kits in volumes  in Q326. Both

suppliers noted they have witnessed stronger than expected strength for NVDA GB as
well as HGX platform in H126; and picking up strong momentum from enterprise
customers as well. The supply chain continues to point to robust AI server growth and
healthy general server demand into H226; consistent with UBS expectations for King
Slide and Chenbro AI server volumes to take off strongly into Sep/Q426, backed by their
leading positions in key GPU and ASIC platforms, with favourable content uplifts.

Other highlights from the day one hardware meetings
1) ZDT anticipates optical module and server business will strengthen from Q226 and
more than doubled in 2026 (optical module/server/IC substrate sales mix  up from 12%
in 2025 to 20%+/45-50% in 2026/2030F). It is building out its high-end PCB capacity,
investing NT$80bn in 2026 with more strategic investments in 2027/2028 to focus on
iHDI/mSAP, HLC, IC substrates, and advanced FPCs, with production to come online in

phases through 2030. 2) Nan Juen noted Eagle Stream remain the mainstream this year
but Birch Stream platform has started to take off in Q2/Q3. Small volumes of Vera Rubin
rail kits started in Q3  with potential ASIC project to kick off in Q4. 3) Chenbro reiterated
+40% YoY 2026 sales target and remained optimistic on 2027 outlook  supported by
strong chassis momentum from both CSPs/enterprises and rack business expansion
(guiding rack sales contribution from 10-15% in 2026 to 15%+ in 2027F). 4)  Lenovo

targets to outgrow its view for 10%-20% YoY industry PC unit decline while data center
business is seeing strong momentum from both CSP and ESMBs with strong AI order
backlog and ISG restructuring move demonstrating strong margin expansion. 5)
Compal sees stable general server demand and AI server picking up streams from
neocloud and enterprise customers while seeing some progress with hyperscalers. It
expects 2026 PC shipments to decline mid teens YoY with consumer models under

pressure into H226 and seeing de-spec on SSD/DRAM for price-sensitive products. 6)
Inventec saw strong growth across AI servers (B300 and higher rack business) and
general servers including two top ASIC CPU projects; and expects NB sales to grow
double digits YoY in 2026F as it volumes outgrow industry (down HSD YoY vs. market
units down teens) on higher commercial exposure (60-70% mix) and higher ASPs.
Stock implications
We stay positive on hardware/component suppliers that commands leading position

### Page 2

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Investing in the technology sector involves a high degree of risk. Rapid technological changes,
increasing competition and exposure to macroeconomic cycles are among the many risks
faced by investors in technology stocks. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the
financial results of tech companies since their operating models are highly volatile and
unpredictable. Finally, valuing technology stocks can prove challenging, as neither traditional

nor non-traditional valuation measures have provided much insight into how these stocks
trade. Valuation methodologies in the sector include target price-book-multiples based on
the long term average ROE and the cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
WIN Semiconductors
2026 Taiwan Summit: Optical and Infrastructure
to drive mid-to-long-term growth
Q326 revenue tracking in line with guidance
WIN Semi attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. August revenue of NT
$1,937mn (+2% QoQ and +31%YoY) is tracking at 65% of both UBSe/Street at +12%
QoQ. WIN Semi maintained its Q326F revenue guidance of low-teens QoQ  growth,
mainly driven by optical and infrastructure segments. It noted revenue outlook from

WiFi has improved from a previously flattish QoQ  to sequentially QoQ growth as a result
of stronger router demand, and cellular remains flattish. Management did not provide
Q426 guidance, noting that demand will partly depend on smartphone seasonality,
although it remains more constructive on the growth outlook for aerospace and non-
smartphone optical markets. In terms of  capex, WIN Semi budgeted NT$2-3bn of capex
spending in 2026, primarily for GaN and InP expansion.

Laser diode remains in qualification but photodiode ramp accelerates
Management noted that its CW lasers and EML remains under  qualification with more
meaningful revenue contribution from 2027 and beyond. While WIN Semi is engaging
with a broader customer base for laser diode than for photodiode, management noted
its near-term optical growth should be driven primarily by the ongoing photodiode
ramp, with volumes expected to increase sequentially both MoM and QoQ through

H226F and continue strengthening into 2027F. Management also noted that its 50G
and 100G VCSEL solutions are already in production, providing additional support for
non-smartphone optical growth. As a result, smartphone-related optical is expected to
decline from 60-70% of optical revenue historically to around 50% in Q326F as non-
smartphone optical applications expand. Management maintained its target for AI/
datacom revenue (driver, PIC, laser diode, photodiode, and etc) exposure to reach high-

single digits by end-2026, up from a low-single-digit contribution in 2025.
Rising aerospace content strengthens long-term growth outlook
Management remains constructive on the long-term outlook and its margin profile as
the business mix shifts toward higher-margin optical and aerospace applications.
Aerospace revenue has increased to over 30% of infrastructure revenue (Infrastructure
was 30-35% of total sales in Q226F), driven by expanding LEO deployments, direct-to-

cell connectivity, and rising RF content as additional frequency bands and advanced
phased-array antennas are adopted, as well as migration from GaAs to GaN platform.
Management views aerospace and optical datacom as key medium- to long-term
growth drivers, supporting a more diversified revenue mix and improved margin profile.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating with a PE-based price target of NT$500
We rate WIN Semi with  BUY and  PT of NT$500, based on 40x 2027-28E average PE. We

believe WIN Semi is well positioned to capture rising infrastructure and optical growth.

Company Description
Established in 1999, WIN Semiconductors is a leading foundry for compound semiconductor
wafers globally, with 43k 6-inch wafer capacity per month as of end-2022. While it offers
different types of compound semiconductors (GaAs, GaN, InP, etc.), the GaAs compound
contributes the majority of its revenue. Its products are used to make signal power amplifiers

that customers use in networking equipment and communication devices, including base
stations and smartphones.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value WIN Semi using PE relative to its historical trading range and peer average.
Investing in WIN Semi involves risks that include, but are not limited to, rapid technological
changes, increasing competition, exposure to macroeconomic cycles and uncertain
enddemand. Slower wireless technology migration and poor performances by customers

could also significantly affect WIN Semi’s fundamental performance. WIN Semi’s LEO satellite
customers may face execution challenges in launching satellites, which will impact WIN
Semi's infrastructure revenue growth outlook. On the other hand, its progress on developing
and qualifying the optical communication laser diodes will have significant impact to its
earnings outlook and market sentiment. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the

financial results of tech companies, since their operating models are highly volatile and
unpredictable.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
